cand.lower().strip()\n        if key in lower_map:\n            return lower_map[key]\n    raise KeyError(f'None of {candidates} found in {list(df.columns)}')\n\nfac_col = find_col(ev_df, final_df.iterrows():\n    key = (str(r[fac_col]).strip(), str(r[sess_col]).strip(),\n           str(r[bin_col]).strip())\n    lookup[key] = float(r[count_col])\n\nprint('\\nLookup dict:')\nfor k, v in lookup.items():\n    print('  ', k, '->', v)\n\n# build formatted rows from plan (preserve order)\nfmt_rows = []\nfor _, r in plan_df.iterrows():\n    fac = str(r['Facility']).strip()\n    sid = str(r['Session ID']).strip()\n    bid = str(r['Bin ID']).strip()\n    pid = r['Product ID']\n    exp = float(r['Expected Qty'])\n    var = float(r['Allowed Variance'])\n    appr = str(r['Approval Needed']).strip().upper()\n    key = (fac, sid, bid)\n\n    missing = 0 if key in lookup else 1\n    gap = 0\n    if key in lookup and appr == 'YES':\n        cnt = lookup[key]\n        if abs(exp - cnt) > var:\n            gap = 1\n    total = missing + gap\n    if missing and gap:\n        summary = 'Missing Final Count, Approval Gap'\n    elif missing:\n        summary = 'Missing Final Count'\n    elif gap:\n        summary = 'Approval Gap'\n    else:\n        summary = 'None'\n\n    fmt_rows.append({\n        'Facility': fac,\n        'Session ID': sid,\n        'Bin ID': bid,\n Qty',\n    'Allowed Variance', 'Approval Needed', 'Missing Final Count',\n    'Approval Gap', 'Total Errors', 'Error Summary'])\n\nprint('\\nFormatted
